# Evidence ledger

Every compatibility claim should be reviewable without trusting an analyst's
memory. Store compact, redistributable summaries in Git; keep full REA output,
snapshots, and target-derived material under ignored `.work/`.

## Record shape

Each investigation record should contain:

- stable record ID and date;
- question being answered;
- artifact path role, byte size, and SHA-256;
- tool and provider versions plus exact command or REA operation;
- observations, with Evidence IDs when REA returns them;
- inferences and their confidence;
- coverage limits and unknowns;
- next smallest query that could resolve each important unknown;
- affected reconstruction obligations.

## Vocabulary

- **Observed:** directly parsed from the identified artifact or captured in a
  controlled run.
- **Derived:** deterministically computed from observations, such as a hash or
  decoded header field.
- **Inferred:** an interpretation supported by observations but not directly
  established by them.
- **Unknown:** not established within the stated search or runtime boundary.

Decompiler pseudocode is an observation produced by a particular analysis
profile. Names, source structure, types, intent, and runtime reachability can
still be inferred or unknown.

| `rea.official-eu.bitmap-loader` | observed | `FUN_00499320` reads Windows BMP header fields directly, accepts 4/8/24-bit input, aligns source rows, walks positive-height images bottom-up, and writes through a locked surface. Its 8-to-8-bit path either copies indices or maps indices 1–255 to the nearest active-palette entry 1–255 by Manhattan RGB distance; index 0 remains 0, exact matches stop the search, and equal-distance ties retain the lower index. Mapping requires the adapter-derived field at `*(PTR_DAT_0050283c + 0x28)` to be zero and flag `0x80` to be clear. Evidence `ev_369eb7072e0370285398eb132c7560351aaebcc7c030e7c234c3af8554dac304`, `ev_b8a2b07dbc450fa42f70b9983eeece49013e8b19e5eaa29b1413daacbd1fd066`, and instruction Evidence `ev_7dd6593ca4c401cb3fdf5e5627585441b800b92433bdf0768a56049b6a5a0b3f`. | This field is distinct from the render context's cached palette beginning at its own `+0x28`. The reconstruction supports the observed 8-bit startup subset and represents the still-unnamed choice explicitly as identity versus nearest-color mapping. Other original conversion/flag paths remain unsupported. Indirect DirectDraw calls are identified from ABI shape, not symbols. |
| `rea.official-eu.bitmap-16bit-source-conversion` | observed | The championship/root loader sends `select.bmp`, R_INFO, the 14 canonical manufacturer-logo requests, and ten groups of 15 numbered preview frames through `FUN_00499320` with flags `0x81`; separate reviewed-corpus metadata identifies the named files used by this slice as 8-bit BMPs with 256-entry embedded palettes. At a 16-bit destination, bit `0x80` suppresses the ordinary active-palette remap, the decoder copies the BMP's embedded BGR entries into a zero-filled RGBX wrapper palette, and every stored source index is expanded through that palette. Display-format discriminator byte `5` packs `((R >> 3) << 10) | ((G >> 3) << 5) | (B >> 3)` (RGB555); every other byte packs `((R >> 3) << 11) | ((G >> 2) << 5) | (B >> 3)` (RGB565). The decoder initially sets the source color-key low and high words to the correspondingly packed embedded-palette entry zero, which need not be numeric zero; later draw helpers can overwrite it, as the Selection Stretch path does with literal `0..0`. At an 8-bit destination the decoder instead retains indices and key `0..0`, leaving displayed colors dependent on the active display palette. Evidence call paths `ev_3d431192309f5947b393aba526b44506e47f847a8e245dc3df740550aa958f13` and `ev_9a26e617a6593a0b9c65ccb7c0da5386041b4e9a6dca74386df329077d0b596b`; decoder `ev_5cde349cec92f4ca7ed830284a6030e50e6c8e060e6ef07b1c6588e9068eddcf`. | Static analysis establishes logical 16-bit words, not native byte order, pitch, alignment, dithering, DirectDraw stretch output, successful driver calls, or final host pixels. The numeric discriminator value has no recovered enum name. Decoder flags alone do not prove a file's source depth, and the general 4-/24-bit branches remain open. The reviewed Chequered palettes equal FCHE in all RGB entries, but that is corpus-specific; R_INFO has no exact external-PAL match and must not be assigned an invented global palette. |

| `rea.official-eu.display-init` | observed | `FUN_00497be0` enumerates adapters/modes, creates DirectDraw through `FUN_00498020`, selects a bounded mode index, stores width/height/depth, and in the 8-bit branch creates primary/back surfaces, creates a 256-entry palette with numeric flags `0x44`, attaches it to both surfaces, and uploads all entries. Windowed mode uses cooperative flags `0x408` and creates a primary plus separate offscreen surface. Fullscreen uses flags `0x451`, calls `SetDisplayMode(width,height,depth,0,0)`, and creates a flipping primary chain with one attached backbuffer. Startup calls the initializer with mode index `2`, flags `1`, and the selected legal/loading palette before decoding and copying the BMP to both destinations. | `rea.official-eu.blit` | observed | `FUN_0049e3e0` chooses fast versus stretch from original rectangle sizes, treats a fully clipped destination as success/no-op, and clips only against destination bounds. Each clipped destination edge shifts the corresponding source edge by the same literal pixel delta, even for stretch. Optional source color key produces observed flags `0x11` for fast or `0x01008000` for stretch; unkeyed flags are `0x10` and `0x01000000`. Evidence `ev_721aab108dc76dc1e6e4e247552dcea7f1fc56b1ad26a5fd9d1e864b6c295ca3` and exact assembly `ev_275158c51689e978e0f1ed161ef525b73bda580338d596d92ea373842634d4c6`. | No source-bound clipping is recovered. DirectDraw method identities are ABI inferences; call arithmetic, flags, dispatch, and success branches are observed statically. |
| `rea.official-eu.championship-selection-stretch` | observed | `FUN_004c0b70` loads `images\\select\\select.bmp` through `FUN_00499320(..., flags=0x81)`. The setup row renderer submits the full source rectangle `(0,0,256,256)` and destination `(base_x-scaled(0x051e), base_y-2, measured_text_width+scaled(0x0f5c)+scaled(0x0a3d), 33)`; at 640×480 this is `(base_x-12, base_y-2, measured_text_width+63, 33)`. It passes key low/high `0/0`, keyed `1`, and wait `1` to the common blitter. Immediately before Stretch, that helper overwrites the surface color key to literal numeric `0..0`, then invokes the destination-surface vtable `+0x14` with numeric flags `0x01008000` and a null effects pointer. This draw-time overwrite is distinct from the decoder's earlier packed-palette-entry-zero key; the reviewed Selection palette entry zero happens to be black, so both values coincide for this artifact. Evidence loader `ev_7e9b6a39c4404864331e2cdfbff8fcf3206ffe4742eec31724d6678a5d239ff2`, caller chain `ev_89df71e30666dcd6a9d69e588dff01c87686e4ed28bb58af621b98adda3c5ba3` and `ev_bd829a8635be12ebbaa009d556bf71ac5fb5e42d132a78513c53af0b37de31e7`, row renderer `ev_163258f9a86b134d96663a37193f377d14642520d72ec342a88798789a459633` plus exact assembly `ev_07185e4821b3d440f610fabada7eadda14c5a50a6e189f387874e1e960cc9672`, and common blitter `ev_77b5cb79e044dcfdcc0d89d8bc9f866b0d890431f5a51b9625b2c899e6bbf082`. | Vtable method names and DirectDraw symbolic flag names are ABI inferences. The executable supplies no filter choice, effects structure, fractional coordinates, sampling loop, rounding rule, or key/filter interaction; rasterization, dithering, and final pixels remain inside the DirectDraw/runtime/driver boundary. Exact pixel parity therefore requires controlled legacy-runtime capture rather than further static analysis. |
| `assets.startup-formats` | observed | All six startup/legal BMPs parse as 640×480, bottom-up, 8-bit `BI_RGB` images with 256 entries and 307,200 normalized indices. All six inspected PAL files are RIFF `PAL ` version `0x0300` with 256 entries. Loading, loaded, and the three non-English legal BMP palettes match their paired external palettes in all 256 RGB entries; English legal matches only 2/256. | Read-only inspection of the profiled local installation using `inspect_startup_art`; no assets are committed. | `impl.startup-formats` | derived | `cmr-formats` provides bounded startup BMP and RIFF-PAL decoding and retains both logical top-down pixels and exact top-down stored DIB rows. `cmr-core::surface` implements explicit identity and recovered nearest-nonzero palette mapping across the complete stored row, exposing `align4(logical_width)` as the compatibility surface width and pitch so retained BMP padding becomes drawable columns. `cmr-core::blit` implements the recovered rectangle expansion, destination clipping, pre-clipping dispatch choice, color-key flags, and no-op decision. `cmr-core::startup_surface` joins reviewed import lookup, decode, selected active palette, and explicit mapping; synthetic tests exercise this path and nonzero padding end to end. | These remain decode/model/planning slices. Zero surface flags are a corpus-backed reconstruction policy, not a recovered assignment. The mapping mode stays explicit until the gating global field is named. No window, DirectDraw call, presentation timing, or 4/24-bit conversion path is implemented. |
| `impl.indexed-display` | derived | `cmr-core::display` defines a platform-neutral 8-bit backend contract and typed ready state. Initialization tests require device/cooperative setup, fullscreen-only display mode selection, the recovered windowed versus flipping-chain topology, one shared palette attached front then back, and palette upload. Startup-copy tests require a full unkeyed visible-front blit followed by a back blit and temporary-source release; either blit may fail without suppressing the other call or release. Mode dimensions select fast versus stretch without assuming 640×480, and invalid depth/extents/source layouts are rejected before backend calls. `cmr-core::startup_display` joins the verified store to that contract: it parses the palette, initializes and reapplies it, then loads the bitmap and performs both copies. A synthetic integration test locks the complete call transcript and proves a failed second palette upload occurs before a deliberately missing bitmap is accessed. | The startup copy deliberately has no separate `present`/`flip` operation: current evidence establishes the visible-primary blit, while steady-frame presentation is a separate recovered path. Initialization failure cleanup and the meaning of startup flag `1` remain unresolved. Persistent resources are backend-owned and released on backend drop. |
| `validation.startup-surface` | observed | A clean V2-profile import of the user-owned corpus loaded both English startup surfaces through `AssetStore`: legal decoded as 640×480 with all 307,200 stored indices changed by nearest-color remapping, while loading decoded as 640×480 with zero changed indices. | Local smoke test only; the temporary imported corpus was removed afterward and no game asset bytes or generated derivatives are retained in Git. The comparison exercises both explicit mapping modes and does not establish which original runtime mode was active. |
| `rea.official-eu.window-loop` | observed | `FUN_0049db00` rejects an existing `Colin McRae Rally` window, creates the game window, hides the cursor, and runs a PeekMessage/GetMessage loop without `TranslateMessage`. `WM_ACTIVATEAPP` dispatch can be suppressed during setup. Inactive or one-shot forced-wait state blocks in `WaitMessage`; otherwise the loop calls the frame tick. The WndProc handles paint, close, erase-background, activation, cursor, four exact system commands, and two palette messages; `WM_CLOSE` is swallowed rather than initiating exit. Activation stores the active flag, restores lost surfaces on activation, and conditionally marks rebuild state on deactivation. Evidence main loop `ev_a09880a02be35b9ab1e6d9f9751a6fbc9297eb1e7b858d2c61538471071a245e`, window creation `ev_0579000d054e606f6f49772afa4d909f86d2b9f30c718901db40a875feb96146`, exact WndProc disassembly `ev_0168e00763161e5ba423d9be8bf9eed1ae321f94e0b6366eefbd9a4de828b83f`, dispatch table `ev_a27b13f380c3f0d7656c260ca8f7bbb42be84b177a62ae37712ed419db67c25c`, and restore path `ev_34e15f90f630a64495fd01e73ec7a1bcad94caccccdacd47791fe715b35efb17`. | Static analysis only. Win32 symbolic message/style names are decoded from observed numeric values. Actual synchronous message order, user-facing quit initiation, and the semantic names of several globals remain unresolved. |
| `rea.official-eu.frame-present` | observed | A completed steady active frame calls `FUN_00498660` once. Fullscreen samples a back-buffer pixel and then invokes the primary surface method inferred as `Flip(NULL, 1)`. Windowed mode resolves the client rectangle/screen origin and calls the DirectDraw method inferred as `WaitForVerticalBlank(1, NULL)`; after a latched failure it polls VGA status port `0x3da` for a blank edge. | `rea.official-eu.championship-car-scene-submit` | observed | A mode-8 car creates one actor per group-0 record. Signed field `+4 == -1` parents the first actor to the selected preview root; otherwise the field selects a previously created actor. Each local 16-word Q16 matrix is copied to the actor, child actors append in source order, and visible traversal is depth-first. Combined row-vector matrices use `child_local * parent_world`: every signed-i64 product is arithmetically shifted by 16, narrowed to its low i32, and wrapping-added; signed i32 components are then scaled by binary64 `2^-16` and stored once as f32. Group-0 values are mesh levels, active level starts at zero, and each visible active mesh submits source-ordered selector batches as numeric `(primitive=4, vertex_type=2, flags=8)`. Selector `-1` requests material and texture zero; nonnegative selector changes bind material then texture through the scene-global cache. The normal frame orders preview callbacks and palette request before the shared depth/scene pass, then clears all car/root visibility latches, commits the pending 8-bit palette, and presents. Evidence loader/hierarchy `ev_78cdaa3880e4d4afe6f25d3addcbb872e57923ffa23688ef98abf456470cad44`, `ev_36d4b7d362524dfdd534fb3b9affa722401fd223908c67c5fccd02dc7a093c03`, and `ev_d3698bb7d0052e4201be00188a24575fe7a413aa814c0b2ecd7ba1f9045f4602`; matrix composition/conversion `ev_80296b33ccdc927d0f4dac0fcdc8961904604876ad4f68a660d68c0dd97c5d06` and `ev_7f39a332b64d2c6c7380273f63bafcd3742608270c327a1842944f5520f0a6c8`; traversal/draw `ev_2a68e1f69ac5211196ed6a3763ad2c6fb66861664e7ead09f9fc92b352f6a1f9`, `ev_4891577f7a75edb8e10dbbd72ce9a2272ff4b034ae908b008b18af34887c6c61`, and `ev_e10a6a6b67069d8d280a1d3b09caef194fb9abf1c2de554666705074dd8a0435`; terminal order `ev_1994060a23851db4b71dd845f82b9b79365127b88d02ef025e1bdde7357fce2f`, `ev_f6bd79271c30e22a0c98f48204c3132046663c8c28c372341e8251a988afb333`, and `ev_f96c7b43e08eb5a3477a4e6a0a75b5858df261dba4b46f7e65b4df0936034b3c`. | Static analysis establishes the hierarchy, arithmetic, numeric submission, and order, not successful driver output. Selecting one run's complete state, selected-`DDPixelFormat` driver `Load` conversion, native packing, dithering, legacy raster precision, and driver final pixels remain unresolved. Reviewed-corpus metadata finds 16–19 actors per car with one preview root and active level zero; it is not a universal format constraint. |

| `rea.official-eu.font` | observed | Frontend startup loads 16 named `.FON` assets into `0x120`-byte runtime objects. Each file starts with a BMP-like fixed-layout 8-bit uncompressed indexed atlas; the font trailer begins at `0x436 + align4(width@0x12) * height@0x16`. A 24-byte header contains a signed glyph count and recovered height/advance/tracking/gap fields, followed by `count * 16`-byte glyph records. The 256-byte lookup initializes to `0xff`; later duplicate codes win. Text is byte-oriented: mapped glyphs advance by width+tracking, missing bytes contribute nothing, space/caret/underscore have special handling, caret forces a line break, and width exactly equal to the viewport takes the wrapping path. Alignment flags use low nibble 2/3 for center/right and high nibble `0x20`/`0x30` for vertical center/bottom. Evidence initialization `ev_8345ff08fd5af08fc7276b09f3e3482520eef6dda6ce76a593c3ddab7ba28e0f`, startup caller `ev_be5c1919ad25e1a439f2629420021568667ee5910fa1ed645c29baf2cf3c2afd`, parser `ev_6c84c9b9f2921e31c8812920e81f78a7e0745d4c188d16e74db52ae07c84df60`, lookup `ev_6bd3bd2a24ac38cc98b86eac7d9a6d9fa4a3683484f48801165ffc1b3ba00966`, measurement `ev_c7cc86d7a4f3e35866a1093a7fffbdac53ae697dabf44b0a60d80022abdacada`, block height `ev_ac66e6170200677174cee6aea2f62ff2c80cfa4b0611c8e4f0a740880038afb6`, wrapping `ev_f4cc3b71cdb2c19c6a9bef1ebe85478585105405a6ba4c5e238cf2d16f79c6b0`, positioning `ev_0a85e069994aed8a2634f757429c132901550238f7033c2f934e99bd867bdccc`, and glyph blit `ev_34bcba196ecad7b61ccde353a4fd0d7c0f848934d620d08f3523c864224cc78b`. | Parser pointer fields and rendering calls are reconstructed as owned offsets/plans, not copied pointer layouts. | `rea.official-eu.alternate-font-render-path` | observed | All 16 frontend fonts are requested with caller argument `0x80`. The direct gate path adds allocation bit 0 and calls the common bitmap decoder with `0x81`; the initialized alternate gate rewrites the request to `0x11`, and `FUN_00499ef0` derives exact decoder flags `0x90`. That flag-`0x10` source path selects an 8-bit destination when device capability field `+0x6c` is nonzero and otherwise selects 16-bit: one 16-bit branch packs ARGB4444 with alpha nibble zero only for exact RGB black and `0xf` otherwise, while the other packs embedded RGB through device-selected component widths/shifts. The load wrapper `0x4958e0(name,rate,bits,stereo)`, guarded by the init flag, builds a PCM `WAVEFORMATEX` (`wFormatTag=1`, `nChannels=1+bool(stereo)`, `nSamplesPerSec=rate`, `wBitsPerSample=bits`, `nBlockAlign=round(nChannels*bits/8)`, `nAvgBytesPerSec=nBlockAlign*rate`), calls `QSWaveMixOpenWave(session,&desc,0,8)`, stores the returned handle at slot `[counter*0x43]`, copies `name` into the slot record (`0x10c` stride), bumps the slot counter `0xa1f848`, and accumulates a size counter `0xa54708`. The play wrapper `0x495a30(slot,channel,flags)`, guarded by the init flag, looks up the slot handle and, when non-null, records the wave-per-channel at `0xa0bd88[channel]` and calls `QSWaveMixPlayEx(session,channel,0,handle,flags,0)`. Evidence: imports `ev_e1b14748b84e7de704e8a25cdbd760665a00feba4c66dce41ca499a252691670`; callers `ev_dacea03282852c9bf3adf11112ec5b6ec676ff108429d02c8be00a51ae682bab`, `ev_0c23c329b299e73d4f96f9c700c71a370951689814f5cf41af09c29ad0725bc0`, `ev_c3e77a79bb8e1335a66fd0881414ad03c8ac391d87d15b42281d8b3bcb524562`; pseudocode `ev_dda2df051e1a984594c225ee6541b61beac95eb75a6302f25cf8e730d6290c33`, `ev_810c278507223b6ac8bf43016effa447043de21c8204c0d6e642e8e7df5c12a1`, `ev_d6575f008b08fd445d0b4929173328bd496dc9737c90f0412f13404dffb2ff29`. | Inferences: `+0x08`=22050 Hz is the mixer output rate, `+0x14`=30 the maximum simultaneous mixing channels, `+0x24` the game hWnd; the load parameter order is (name, sample-rate, bits-per-sample, stereo-flag); wave slots are a fixed-capacity table (~305 records of 268 bytes) keyed by an incrementing counter holding one handle plus the source name. Unknowns: exact `QSWMIXCONFIG` meaning of fields `+0x04` and `+0x18`; the `0`/`8` `OpenWave` arguments and the `PlayEx` `dwFlags`/trailing argument; whether wave payload bytes come from memory or file in this path; loop/stop/completion semantics (`IsChannelDone`/`StopChannel`) and volume/pan/frequency rules (`SetVolume`/`SetPosition`/`SetFrequency`) are not yet traced; the second load (`0x4959f0`) and play (`0x495a80`) wrapper variants are not yet differentiated. Each registered material record holds: palette slot `+0x00` (into `+0x8c`), own global slot `+0x02` (into `+0x20b4`, placed at first free slot), texture handle `+0x10`, dest surface `+0x14`, system source descriptor `+0x1c` (w/h at `+0x08/+0x0a` alloc, `+0x10/+0x14` used). A source face's group-2 ordinal is loop-1's iteration index; **loop-3 remaps it to the record's global `+0x20b4` slot** and writes that to the runtime face (`face+0x04`), `-1` = untextured. Binding is **per-batch at draw time** (not baked): `FUN_004a28a0` reads the prim ordinal (prim `+0x00`), and in software mode binds the record's palette (`+0x00` -> `[+0x8c]`, `FUN_004a23b0`, device vtable `+0x64`) then texture handle (`+0x10`, `FUN_004a2380`, vtable `+0x5c`), caching the last bound slot at `DAT_00502778`; `-1` binds null palette+texture (texturing off). DrawIndexedPrimitive is device vtable `+0x78` (TRILIST, FVF from LOD sub-header `+0x10`). Blob tag `blob[0]=='A'` (0x41) = composite/animated material = ordered sub-texture-ordinal frame list (`blob+8` u16 pairs; parallel table `DAT_00bb9500` stride 0xb8); other tags are inline paletted (`FUN_0049a2b0`: `byte+0x04 & 3` -> palette size, `&8` inline palette at `+0x14`, power-of-two w/h) or truecolor (`FUN_00499ef0`: bpp `+0x1c`, w/h `+0x12`/`+0x16`). | `rea.official-eu.c3d-inline-texture` | observed | The exact on-disk byte layout of the two C3D group-2 "inline engine" texture blob formats (reconstruction item E03, resolving the `rea.official-eu.c3d-material-binding` unknown about `FUN_00499ec0`'s flags mapping), cross-checked pseudocode against assembly for every critical offset. Each group-2 entry is `{ char name[0x0c]; u32 len; byte blob[len] }`; `blob = entry+0x10` (asm-confirmed strncpy of `0xc` + `u32 len` at `+0xc`). The dispatcher `FUN_00499ec0(blob, flags)` is `if (flags&1) truecolor(FUN_00499ef0); else if (flags&2) paletted(FUN_0049a2b0); else 0` - it does **not** read the tag. The loader `FUN_004a2b70` derives `flags` from the leading blob byte: `blob[0]==0x41` ('A') = composite (handled inline, not dispatched); `blob[0]==0x10` -> `flags=6` (paletted); any other byte -> `flags=5` base (truecolor). The remaining `flags` bits are runtime surface hints (0x4 keep-sysmem, 0x10 caller `param_5&0x10`, 0x20/0x40 texture-reduction, 0x80 mipmaps) and do not change the byte layout. **Paletted (`FUN_0049a2b0`/`FUN_0049a540`):** mode byte at `blob+0x04`; `mode&3` -> palette entry count (0->16, 1->256, 2->0, 3->reject); `mode&8` -> inline palette present at `blob+0x14` (2 bytes/entry LE, X1R5G5B5 with R in the low 5 bits, each channel `<<3`). The dimension descriptor is at `blob+0x08` (no inline palette) or right after the palette (`blob+0x14+count*2`); width `u16 @ descriptor+0x08`, height `@ descriptor+0x0a`, index data `@ descriptor+0x0c`; width/height must be powers of two in `1..=512`. Each row is `2*width` bytes tightly packed (256-colour = 1 byte/pixel; 16-colour = 2 pixels/byte, low nibble first). **Truecolor (`FUN_00499ef0`/`FUN_00499320`):** pixel-data offset `u32 @ blob+0x0a` (pixel data = `blob + that`); width `u16 @ blob+0x12` (used rounded up to a multiple of 4); height `u16 @ blob+0x16`; source bpp `u16 @ blob+0x1c` in {4,8,0x18} else reject; palette-count override `u32 @ blob+0x2e` (if nonzero wins over the bpp default 4->16/8->256); inline palette `@ blob+0x36`, 4 bytes/entry BGRX. 24-bit pixels are 3 bytes/pixel, row stride = width padded to a multiple of 4 pixels; 8-bit = 1 byte/pixel; 4-bit = 2 pixels/byte, high nibble first. **The 'A' composite on-disk part:** `blob+0x04` u8 attribute (stored to the runtime table, not the frame count); frame list at `blob+0x08`, stride 4 (`+0x00` u16 relative sub-texture ordinal, `+0x02` u8 per-frame param); the frame **count** is read from the global byte `DAT_00bb9664`, not the blob. | Static analysis of `.work/official-eu/Rally.exe` (`FUN_00499ec0`/`FUN_0049a2b0`/`FUN_0049a540`/`FUN_00499ef0`/`FUN_00499320`/`FUN_004a2b70`); every blob offset verified directly in `procedure_assembly` (the decompiler's +4 EBP-local shift affects only spill slots, not the `param`-relative blob reads). **Inferences:** `mode&3` encodes palette size *and* source depth; paletted palette is X1R5G5B5 (R low), truecolor palette is BGRX - both inferred from the DirectDraw PALETTEENTRY channel routing; truecolor 24-bit channel order (BGR) is inferred, not byte-proven. **Unknowns:** paletted descriptor bytes `+0x00..+0x07` and `blob[1..3]`; most truecolor header bytes outside the six consumed fields (incl. width/height upper halves); the exact row padding of 4/8-bit truecolor; the 'A' frame-count source (`DAT_00bb9664`'s writer not located) and the `blob+0x04` attribute meaning. No payload bytes transcribed; decompiler observation of a user-owned binary under `.work/`. |
| `impl.c3d-engine-texture-parser` | derived | `cmr-formats::c3d` adds a pure, bounds-checked parser for the two engine inline texture blob formats (`rea.official-eu.c3d-inline-texture`). `parse_c3d_engine_bitmap(blob) -> Result<C3dEngineBitmap, C3dEngineBitmapError>` dispatches on the leading byte exactly as the loader does (`C3D_ENGINE_PALETTED_TAG = 0x10` -> paletted, else truecolor), with `parse_c3d_paletted_bitmap`/`parse_c3d_truecolor_bitmap` as standalone entry points and `C3dBlobRecord::engine_bitmap()` as a convenience. `C3dEngineBitmap::{Paletted(C3dPalettedBitmap), Truecolor(C3dTruecolorBitmap)}` frames (does not expand) the payload: borrowed dimensions, inline palette, and pixel/index region, with `inline_palette_entry(i) -> [R,G,B]` decoding X1R5G5B5 (paletted) / BGRX (truecolor). The admitted scalar is `qmul(qmul(delta,0x4000),qmul(DAT_0050c5c4,0x1999))` (`0x40f691..0x40f6c3`). Its wrapping absolute value is compared signed to `+0x35c[wheel]`; strictly above selects signed `+0x36c[wheel]`, equality retains (`0x40f6c7..0x40f6f4`). The second rule subtracts the wrapping absolute vector projection from an upstream scalar budget, proceeds only for a signed-positive remainder, bounds the first scalar's wrapping absolute value by that remainder, and uses a positive sign only for a strictly positive projection (`0x40f788..0x40f80a`). A game wave name is mapped to a relative path under the root with backslashes normalised to separators and unsafe requests refused (no absolute/drive paths, no `.`/`..`/empty components, no NUL - mirroring `cmr-assets::resolver`), media is read in place, and the target must be a regular file (symlinks/special files refused). It delegates the recovered bookkeeping to `MixerSessionState` (init guard, sequential slots, unknown-slot rejection, null-handle no-op, channel-wave record) and adds per-channel voices with one-shot vs looping (`PLAY_LOOPING`), a `frequency/output_rate` playback step, nearest-neighbour resample, and mono/stereo frame handling. Tuning block @`0x4ebe00` (stride `0x28`, name[0x20]+value, text-overridable): `Track_Decay_Life=0x4d8`, `Track_Decay_RATE=0x7c`, `Draw_dirt_my_viewport=100`/`other=30`, `Gas/Loose_sand_alpha_delta=3`, `Smoke/Dry_snow=2`, `Spray_wetness=8`, `Splash_wetness=0x28`. **Shadow** (`FUN_004a4f20` init / `FUN_004a51f0` build, "Cm" shadows): 64x64 blob (`images\shadpc.bmp`+`shadam.bmp`), grey `(100,100,100,170)` soft-edged projected polygons (per-vertex alpha `0.0/0.3/0.5/0.7/1.0`, ground plane Q16.16 `-0x20000`, Q16.16->float `*1.5258789e-05`), index tables `DAT_00b73420`(11)/`DAT_00b73890`(15), gated by detail `8`. **Fog is software distance-haze** (`FUN_004afe60` color / `FUN_004b0160` density `0.2/0.4/0.6/0.8` / `FUN_004b01b0` far `20/40/60/70/80` Q16.16 / `FUN_004afee0` disable, keyed by weather+intensity+country in `FUN_0048b960`) - no DX fog render-state string; `.hzn` horizon via `FUN_00480d80` suppressed when `DAT_00d3ca80+0x25 & 0x80`; full-screen precip overlay `IMAGES\SNOW.BMP` `(100,100,100,255)` for weather 3/4. | Static analysis of `.work/official-eu/Rally.exe`. The per-surface capability tables, sprite-sheet image sets, and the exact weather/intensity->fog-value selection are game/asset data. The `Max_dust_size` comparison scale and the billboard record field offsets beyond type/frame are partially inferred. |
| `impl.weather-effects` | derived | `cmr-core::weather` reconstructs the testable environmental behavior: state gates `country_index`/`is_wet_weather` (3\|4)/`is_low_detail` (==8); `accumulate_effect_enable` summing a caller-supplied per-surface capability table over the distinct present surface ids (effect on iff counter > 0); `particles` layout constants + `particle_effect` type dispatch (dust `0x01`/`0x06`/`0x09`/`0x16`/`0x18`, clods `0x02`, snow `0x07`, snow-cloud `0x08`, splash `0x0b`, leaves `0x0c`, gravel/dry-mud `0x15`/`0x17`/`0x1a`), `initial_anim_frame` (three-byte average or `0x32`), `is_visible` cull window, `follow_size` growth+clamp, `particle_alpha` scale+low-detail floor; `tuning` named defaults; `shadow` color/soft-edge-alpha/ground-plane/`fixed_to_float` + `ShadowPrimitive` geometry (4-vert strip / 11- / 15-index); `sky` fog color/density/far constant sets + `loads_horizon` gate. Fog is treated as the engine-level analog of the C3D type-2 per-vertex fog (E02). 10 unit tests cover the gates, aggregation (distinct/empty/out-of-range), type dispatch, frame average vs floor, cull boundaries, size growth/clamp, alpha saturation/floor, byte-exact tuning, shadow primitives + Q16.16 conversion, and the horizon gate + fog constants. | The per-surface capability tables and sprite-sheet filename sets are game data and not vendored (tables are caller-supplied). No rendering path yet - this is domain behavior consumed later by the C3D raster / modern backend. |

| `impl.image-comparison-harness` | derived | `cmr-test-support::image_compare` is the differential-verification primitive (E11) for the reconstruction order's "verify behavior with differential fixtures" step. `Rgb888Image` holds a validated `width*height` canonical-RGB buffer; `Tolerance` models a per-channel absolute delta plus an accepted outlier-pixel fraction, with presets `EXACT` (bit-exact), `RGB565_ROUNDTRIP` (per-channel delta 8 - a 5-bit channel widened by bit-replication differs from its 8-bit source by at most 7, so quantization produces no outliers), and `dithered(fraction)`. `compare_rgb888` fails fast on a dimension mismatch, then walks every pixel and reports the max channel delta, the outlier count, the first-outlier coordinate, and the pass/fail verdict. `expand_channel`/`rgb565_to_canonical` reproduce the standard bit-replication widening so a packed 5-6-5 framebuffer can be diffed against an 8-bit oracle. Fixtures (`horizontal_red_ramp`/`checkerboard`/`shifted`) are deterministic source-owned patterns. 9 tests cover count/dimension rejection, exact pass/fail with first-outlier reporting, the quantization round-trip bound, the dithered outlier fraction, channel expansion, 5-6-5 decode, and the empty image. | This is a source-owned comparison metric, not a legacy-driver pixel oracle; it is deliberately decoupled from `cmr-video` so any backend is held to the same metric. Concrete per-subsystem reference captures and the exact tolerance accepted for each render path are produced as that path is converted (none asserted here); RGBA, structural/perceptual metrics, and sub-pixel alignment are out of scope for this primitive. |

| `impl.present-scaling` | derived | `cmr-core::present_scaling` is the backend-neutral presentation-scaling contract (B11): `LogicalSize`/`PhysicalSize`/`Viewport` and the pure `fit_viewport(logical, physical, scaling)` placement math under a `PresentationScaling` policy (`Stretch` fills; `Letterbox` is the largest aspect-preserving fractional fit, centered; `IntegerScale` is the largest aspect-preserving integer fit clamped to >=1, centered). `DEFAULT_SCALING` is `IntegerScale`, matching the `SDL_LOGICAL_PRESENTATION_INTEGER_SCALE` the SDL presenter was already using. The `cmr-platform-sdl3` presenter selects its SDL logical-presentation mode through `logical_presentation(DEFAULT_SCALING)`, so the backend consumes the project-defined policy rather than a hardcoded constant; a later GPU backend sets its viewport from `fit_viewport` and both are checked against the same worked examples. 8 geometry tests (exact-multiple fill, largest-fitting-multiple centering, 1x clamp + negative-origin overflow, fractional letterbox with aspect preserved, stretch, pillarbox vs letterbox orientation, empty frame, default policy) + 1 SDL mapping test. | This is reconstruction policy for the modern backend, not a decompiled original formula - the shipped game flips a logical-resolution back buffer with no window scaling. The geometry mirrors SDL's integer-scale logical presentation so the SDL backend and a future GPU backend agree; live physical-size queries, fullscreen toggling on the running window, and the SDL window/event lifecycle itself are backend glue exercised only under a display server. |

| `impl.host-lifecycle` | derived | `cmr-core::host_lifecycle::HostLifecycle` composes the recovered host loop (`host_loop`, B09) and window procedure (`window`) into the end-to-end native lifecycle (B12). The `cmr-platform-sdl3` adapter surfaces the events (`HostEvent::FocusGained`/`FocusLost` from SDL `WindowEvent::FocusGained`/`FocusLost`/`Minimized`, `HostEvent::as_lifecycle` bridging quit/focus, `HostEventState::focused`). 7 lifecycle contract tests + 2 SDL adapter tests. | Reconstruction-policy decision for the modern backend: a platform close request drives the loop quit flag because the recovered `WM_CLOSE` is swallowed and does not itself initiate exit. The live game host loop will consume `HostLifecycle` once the full loop is SDL-driven (I-series); runtime fullscreen toggling and the SDL event pump itself are backend glue exercised only under a display server. |

| `rea.official-eu.settings-paths` | observed | Settings persistence and path resolution (C05). The saver `FUN_004ba5e0` writes the full `0x1e20` blob unless `blob[0x08] & 1` is set. I/O helpers: `FUN_00495ed0` (open/read; probes a CMPR archive via `&DAT_0050234c` then falls back to Win16 `OpenFile`+`GetFileSize`+`ReadFile`, read OK only if bytes==size; `mode==1` HD base `DAT_00a1f954`, `mode==2` CD base `DAT_00a1fa5c` behind an ESC-cancellable insert-CD wait `FUN_00496ca0`) and `FUN_00496580` (write; `OpenFile` with `OF_CREATE` 0x1000, no header/checksum). Paths are **registry-driven**: `FUN_00496b50` opens `HKLM`(0x80000002)`\SOFTWARE\Codemasters\Rally\1.00` and `RegQueryValueExA` for `CDPath`/`HDPath` (`0x4eb924`/`0x50863c`), `FUN_00496740` reads `Hardware` (`0x5023a0`, seeds `blob[0x12]` when no file loads); resolvers `FUN_004c31a0` (front-end: `CDPath\new_fe` else `q:\new_fe`; HD: `HDPath` else `c:\codemast\cmrally`) and `FUN_0043bb60` (in-game: `CDPath\game` else `Q:\Game`); path setters `FUN_00496670`/`FUN_004966d0` append `\` and MessageBox+abort on a null path. Saves: `SAVE\SAVESLOT` (`0x4f1290`) + `<n>` + `.cms` (`0x4f12a0`), loader `FUN_0044edac` reads 12 slots of exactly `0x6ac` bytes (wrong size -> zeroed/empty), browser `FUN_0044f2f0` shows "EMPTY SLOT" (`0x4f12b0`) for a missing file. | Static analysis of `.work/official-eu/Rally.exe`. Registry IS used (read-only; no `RegSetValueExA`/`RegCreateKeyExA` seen - installer presumably writes the keys). `find_xrefs_to_name` was unreliable (returned not-found for demonstrably-called `RegOpenKeyExA`), so the absence of `GetModuleFileNameA`/`getenv`/`GetDriveTypeA`/`FindFirstFileA` is not positively proven, only unneeded. `CONTROL.DAT` (`0x5025ec`) and `m:\jonw\points.dat` (`0x4f3ec0`) have no code xrefs (dead strings); a separate high-score file was not located. | `impl.native-startup-config` | derived | `cmr-app` threads the C05 portable settings/path backend through native startup (C13). `AppOptions` parses `--source <media root>` with the `CMR_ORIGINAL_DIR` environment fallback (`SOURCE_ENV`) - the portable replacement for the shipped registry `CDPath`/`HDPath` resolution - resolved optionally: an explicit option wins over the environment, an empty value is the typed `ConfigError::EmptySource`, and absence is allowed (the headless root-frame demo needs no media). `RuntimeConfig` exposes `media_root`/`media_source` and `data_paths()`, which pairs the writable runtime-data root with the media root into a `settings_store::DataPaths`. `resolve_startup_settings(Option<&[u8]>)` reproduces the recovered load gate over injected file bytes: `None` -> `Defaults`, exactly `SETTINGS_BLOB_LEN` -> `Loaded` with `clear_runtime_region` applied, wrong length -> `Rejected(WrongLength)`. 5 tests (source precedence/fallback, optional media + empty-source rejection on both sources, duplicate `--source`, the three settings-gate outcomes). | Reconstruction policy, not a decompiled sequence: the shipped registry/absolute-path startup is replaced by explicit roots. | `impl.interactive-native-root-lifecycle` | derived | `cmr-platform-sdl3::advance_root_lifecycle` wires the B12 host lifecycle into the interactive native root-menu loop (D18). | `rea.official-eu.save-slots` | observed | Save-slot/profile persistence (F03). A save is a single `0x6ac`-byte record written whole by `FUN_0044ef50` via `FUN_00496580` (`OpenFile` with `OF_CREATE` 0x1000, then `WriteFile` - truncate+overwrite, no positioning) to `SAVE\SAVESLOT<n>.cms` under the writable base; 12 slots. `FUN_0044ef50(slot)` (called from save menu `FUN_0043bce0` case 7, cursor `DAT_00551b5c`): allocates `0x6ac`, copies `0x1a4` dwords (`0x690`) from `*(DAT_00d3ca80+0x2c)` to record `+0x0c`, stores trailing scalars `record+0x69c`=`*(DAT_00d3ca80+0x20)`, `+0x6a0`=`+0x24`, `+0x6a4`(u16)=`+0x28`, zeroes `0x54` bytes at `+0x1c`, writes magic `0x12348765` at `+0x04` and const `0x690` at `+0x08`, sets `+0x6a6`=`(*(byte*)(DAT_00d3ca80+0x18)>>3)&3`, builds a seed at `+0x00` from three `FUN_004cfac0` RNG words (`((r0<<10)^r1)<<10 ^ r2 | 0xAA000000`) and `+0x6a8`=`~seed`, then writes all `0x6ac` bytes. Validity/occupancy `FUN_0044eed0(slot)` over `DAT_0055d74c + slot*0x6ac`: `+0x04==0x12348765 && +0x08==0x690 && +0x6a8==~(+0x00)`. Loader `FUN_0044ed90` allocates `DAT_0055d74c` (`0x5010`=12x`0x6ac`), per slot reads the whole file (`FUN_00495ed0`), and copies it in only when the size is exactly `0x6ac` (else zeroes the record), then builds flag table `DAT_0055c248` (12 ints) and display-line table `DAT_0055c298` (12 x `0x104`); `FUN_0044f210` computes a 3-char standings tag into `+0x0c`; a `+0x150` sub-record array (stride `0x10`, flag bit `0x10` at `+8`) holds progress. | Static analysis of `.work/official-eu/Rally.exe`. **No delete primitive** (`DeleteFileA`/`_unlink`/`remove` all `name_not_found`; no delete/unlink strings) and **no payload checksum** (the `~seed` complement covers only the 4-byte seed). The validity triple doubles as a format-version gate (const `0x690` = payload length). There is no free-text profile name: identity is the championship snapshot; the `+0x0c` tag is derived, not entered. The slot->runtime load-apply is dispatched indirectly (function pointer) and was not statically pinned; `+0x6a7` is unwritten padding; the `0x690` payload and the `+0x150` sub-record semantics are game data, not decoded. |
| `impl.profile-store` | derived | `cmr-core::profile_store` reconstructs the save-slot record's structural header and flows. Constants fix the offsets (`SLOT_SEED/MAGIC/FORMAT/PAYLOAD/VOLATILE_ZERO/MODE/COMPLEMENT`), the magic `0x12348765`, the format marker `0x690`, and the `0xAA000000` seed tag. `make_seed(r0,r1,r2)` reproduces the RNG expression, `seed_complement` is `!seed`, `slot_is_valid` checks length+magic+format+complement, `classify_slot(Option<&[u8]>)` reproduces the load gate (`SlotState::Empty` for absent/wrong-size/invalid, `Occupied` for valid), `slot_mode` masks the 2-bit field, and `finalize_slot_for_save` zeroes the volatile window and stamps seed/magic/format/complement so a caller-filled payload becomes a valid record. 8 tests cover the seed tag + determinism + recovered expression, complement round-trip, a finalize->valid stamp with the volatile window zeroed and payload preserved, wrong-length rejection, per-field validity rejection (magic/format/tampered-seed/length), the classify gate (valid/absent/wrong-size/right-size-invalid), the 2-bit mode, and the offset relations. | Structural model only: the `0x690` championship payload is game state and is neither modeled nor vendored (offset/length only). Create and save are the same path (no separate create). There is no delete primitive in the original - a portable host deletes by removing the file, which then classifies Empty; `finalize_slot_for_save` stamps the header but the caller supplies the payload and the slot->runtime apply. |

Entrant count = `DAT_00550c08` (or `2` in 2-player). AI opponent times are generated per stage by `FUN_00465d40` from a per-country car-tier table `&DAT_00506e48` (stride `0x14`, indexed by country) scaled by RNG `FUN_004cfac0` between a base and total time - not table-stored. The path-safety rejection shared by `cmr-core::audio_render::FileWaveSource::safe_path` and `cmr-assets::resolver::normalize_request` is Windows-correct by construction (rejects leading `/`/`\`, drive-qualified `C:` names, and `.`/`..`/empty components after `\`->`/` normalization); both reject-tests are hardened with backslash-form traversal, UNC, and drive-relative inputs. | Verification + test hardening milestone; no new reconstructed behavior. `cargo check --target` type-checks without the mingw linker, so this proves source portability, not a linked Windows binary. The path-safety rule is now a single shared helper (`cmr_assets::normalize_relative_path`) used by both `cmr-assets::resolver` and `cmr-core::audio_render::FileWaveSource` - cmr-core depends on cmr-assets, so the one rule lives at the lower layer and a hardening applies to both. |

The `bundled-sdl3` feature was added to `cmr-platform-sdl3` as a passthrough to `sdl3/build-from-source`, so the SDL jobs build SDL3 from source (CMake + C toolchain only) rather than needing a system library; the default still links system SDL3. | Build/CI infrastructure, no reconstructed behavior. The type-1 (normal-lit) path writes only the untextured `0xff000000` and no per-vertex repack. The finalizer also rescales partially-resident texture UVs (`lvertex+0x18`/`+0x1c`) by the loaded-subrect ratio, deduplicating per vertex ordinal through a 1024-entry visited table. | Static analysis of `.work/official-eu/Rally.exe`. | `rea.official-eu.c3d-view-inverse` | observed | The camera VIEW matrix is the **affine inverse of the camera-world matrix** (`FUN_0048f460`), not a look-at or BAM composition. The matrix is 16 Q16.16 `int` words, column-major: basis columns at word indices `0..=2`/`4..=6`/`8..=10`, translation at `0xc..=0xe`, homogeneous row `0x3`/`0x7`/`0xb = 0`, `0xf = 0x10000`. The routine computes three cofactors `cof0 = m5*m10 - m9*m6`, `cof1 = m9*m2 - m1*m10`, `cof2 = m1*m6 - m5*m2` (each a Q16.16 multiply `(a*b)>>0x10` via a 64-bit intermediate), the determinant `det = cof2*m8 + cof1*m4 + cof0*m0`, and - when `det != 0` - the integer reciprocal `recip = 0x100000000 / det` (an `i64` divide truncated to `i32`). The inverse basis is the adjugate scaled by `recip`: `out0=recip*cof0`, `out1=recip*cof1`, `out2=recip*cof2`, `out4=recip*(m8*m6-m4*m10)`, `out5=recip*(m0*m10-m8*m2)`, `out6=recip*(m4*m2-m0*m6)`, `out8=recip*(m4*m9-m8*m5)`, `out9=recip*(m8*m1-m0*m9)`, `out10=recip*(m0*m5-m4*m1)` (all Q16.16 multiplies). The inverse translation applies the inverse basis to the negated source translation: `out[0xc] = (-m14)*out8 + (-m13)*out4 + (-m12)*out0`, likewise `0xd`/`0xe` over the next basis rows; `out[0xf] = 0x10000`, `out[3]=out[7]=out[0xb]=0`. A singular basis (`det == 0`) calls `FUN_0048ed80`, which writes a Q16.16 identity. The companion transform `FUN_0048fa60` applies a matrix to a point as `out[i] = m[0xc+i] + sum_j ((m[4*j+i]*v[j])>>0x10)`. The per-frame camera setup `FUN_004a7d70` computes this inverse from the camera object's world matrix at `camera+0xd4` into the device context slot `ctx+0x4100`, converts all 16 Q16.16 words to float (`* 1.5258789e-05` = `2^-16`), and calls the device vtable `+0x68` (`SetTransform`) with state `2` = `D3DTRANSFORMSTATE_VIEW`; the projection builder `FUN_004a71b0` likewise uses state `3` and per-node WORLD uses state `1`. **Decisive negative: there is no CPU matrix-matrix concatenation** - WORLD * VIEW * PROJECTION is concatenated by Direct3D per vertex, so a software oracle must perform that concatenation itself. | Static analysis of `.work/official-eu/Rally.exe`. The routine is **pure integer arithmetic** (64-bit multiply reduced by `>>0x10`, one 64-bit reciprocal divide), so it is byte-exact reproducible with no 1990s-driver floating-point dependency - unlike the rasterizer's pixel evaluation. The Q16.16 reciprocal truncates toward zero; exact parity for non-power-of-two determinants follows from the identical integer sequence, not a float tolerance. No camera-spline or per-track transform tables transcribed. |

Wired into the SDL windowed session via the `ViewHarness` wrapper (a key-bound `cycle` + per-frame `update`; see `impl.windowed-game-session`) - only the change-view key and the RNG seed are documented harness policy. | `impl.c3d-occlusion-oracle` | derived | The gameplay-scene exact-pixel visual oracle, proven over the real command path (`cmr-video` test `gameplay_zbuffer_resolves_occlusion_independent_of_draw_order`). Two synthetic C3D objects sharing one drawable command but placed at different node world-Z depths are preflighted with `for_gameplay_scene` matrices (`preflight_root_c3d_copy_batch`) and composited into one shared framebuffer + `SoftwareC3dDepthBuffer` via the real `compose_root_c3d_indexed_copy_no_blend` (no depth clear between draws). The test asserts the **hardware-Z-buffer contract**: (1) the final indexed framebuffer and the depth buffer are **bit-identical regardless of submission order** ([near, far] == [far, near]) - the defining Z-buffer property a painter's triangle sort would not provide; (2) the combined depth at each pixel equals the elementwise `min` of the two objects' solo depth buffers (the nearest surface is retained); (3) where both objects cover a pixel the nearer one is strictly in front and its texel wins; (4) drawing the far object after the near one rejects its occluded fragments (`SoftwareC3dFragmentOutcome::depth_rejected() > 0`, and fewer total fragments pass than the two would independently); (5) the whole sequence is deterministic. This closes the deterministic compatibility renderer/oracle loop (transform -> clip -> 2D-XZ cull -> Z-buffered raster -> multi-object occlusion) end-to-end. | Self-consistency verification against the recovered contract (determinism, draw-order independence, Z-buffer min, occlusion), **not a differential comparison against the shipped binary** - the software raster uses sequential-`f32` by stated reconstruction policy and is not byte-exact to the 1990s DX5 HW rasterizer, and running the original is a scoped runtime experiment deferred under the static-analysis default. The two-object fixture reproduces the C3D format shape only; no game payloads vendored. |

| `rea.official-eu.c3d-scene-node` | observed | The C3D (`CM3D`) scene-graph node format and tree threading, from the parser `FUN_004a2b70` (read in full against pseudocode **and** assembly) and the attach helper `FUN_00492410`. `FUN_004a2b70` validates magic+version (`"CM3D"`, version `100`) then runs three sequential loops: (1) material/texture registration from count B (`u16 @+0x10`, table `+0x1c`), (2) a mesh/surface pointer LUT from the count `@+0x0e` (table `+0x18`, advancing by each block's `+0x0c` size), (3) the node tree from the Group0 transform table, count A (`u16 @+0x0c`, table `+0x14`). Each Group0 record has a **variable stride**: its `+0x00` dword is the total record size (the `0x50` prefix is followed by an inline mesh-index list of `+0x4c` u16 entries at `+0x50`), so the loop advances by that size, not a fixed `0x50`. Loop 3 maps each record 1:1 to a node (record 0 is the root marker `field_04 == -1`, so the "pre-marker geometry" skip is dead and every record becomes a node): it allocates a `0x194`-byte node (`FUN_00492290` -> `FUN_004990d0(0x194)`, registered in the global node table `DAT_00966fac`/count `DAT_0096eff0`), copies the 16-word Q16.16 matrix at record `+0x08` to node `+0x54` **and** `+0x94`, the class/id dword at record `+0x48` to node `+0x2c` (low 12 bits = the id `FUN_00495050` searches by; the whole value `== 0x6f` marks a translucent/deferred node), the flag bytes at record `+0x4d`/`+0x4e` to node `+0x190`/`+0x191`, and node `+0x180` = `3` for a mesh-less transform-only node else `0`. `field_04 == -1` attaches the node under the caller's container (`param_3`); otherwise the parent is `DAT_00966fac[batch_base + field_04]` where `batch_base` freezes `DAT_0096eff0` at loop entry - a **batch-relative, strictly backward** reference. `FUN_00492410(node, parent)` appends the node as the parent's **last** child (first-child `+0x04` when empty, else walk the next-sibling chain `+0x00` to the tail; parent back-ptr `+0x08`); a root (`parent == 0`) instead gets identity matrices at `+0xd4`/`+0x94`. Modes 2/3/5/6/7/8 build the tree (5 = stage, 6 = car); modes 0/1 are instance placement (`DAT_008697a0` table). Node `+0x114` (world matrix) and `+0x184` (render-pass mask) are **not** written at load - runtime/traversal state. | Static analysis of `.work/official-eu/Rally.exe` (pseudocode + `procedure_assembly`; the decompiler's `local_NN` names are shifted +4 from the real EBP offsets, resolved against the assembly). The runtime node is an individual heap allocation (not a contiguous pool); the tree is an intrusive child/sibling list built into that pool, not on the decompressed buffer (freed by the caller). The on-disk gzip/`CMPR` envelope is decompressed before `FUN_004a2b70`; that is the gzip/DEFLATE path already reconstructed in `cmr-formats::stored_asset` (A09), not a bespoke codec. Still open: loops 1/2 mesh/material instantiation and the `5/6/7/8` LOD vertex streams. No geometry/matrix payloads transcribed. |

| `impl.gpu-indexed-palette-conformance` | derived | Phase 4A establishes one API-neutral exact indexed-palette presentation contract and two implementations. `cmr-gpu-contract::IndexedFrameView` validates non-zero extents, exact `width*height` indices, and exactly 256 RGBA palette entries; canonical expansion makes every output alpha byte opaque and ignores palette flag bytes. `cmr-video-wgpu` samples `R8Uint` index and `Rgba8Uint` palette textures with integer `textureLoad`; `cmr-video-sdl-gpu` implements the same pipeline through SDL_GPU forced and runtime-validated as Vulkan. `cmr-gpu-conformance` first proves its contract expansion matches the established `cmr-video::SoftwarePresentedFrame` expansion, then checks exact RGBA readback for both candidates and their cross-backend equality on one asymmetric 257x2 source-owned fixture containing all 256 indices plus a palette-only mutation. Required timings cover the same wall interval in both backends (per-frame allocation/upload preparation through submit, then submit-to-completion); GPU timestamps are supplementary. Focused tests cover validation, expansion, padded RGBA/BGRA normalization, summaries, runner parsing, and established-oracle mismatch reporting. Local offscreen runs passed on the available NVIDIA RTX 5090 and AMD Radeon 610M/RADV Vulkan adapters. | Derived modern-backend conformance evidence, not a recovered rendering claim and not a performance winner. The SDL wrapper does not expose PCI IDs, so `same_vendor_device_pair` is explicitly only a vendor/device tuple comparison and cannot prove unique physical identity. SDL_GPU currently compiles source-owned GLSL through an external `glslangValidator` and supports only Vulkan in this slice; reproducible shader packaging and DX12 shaders remain open. The fixture exercises palette presentation, not native C3D geometry; both candidates must next consume identical raw recovered C3D draw packets and a caller-selected imported stage before I08 or the I09 selection can close. No game payload is included. |

| `impl.gpu-raw-c3d-conformance` | derived | Phase 4B's bounded native-geometry correctness slice. `cmr-gpu-contract` defines validated raw C3D resources (`C3dVertexQ16` preserves all eight signed Q16.16 words; non-empty compact `u16` triangle lists; 1..=256-entry indexed materials), recovered matrix-word draws, submission brackets kept separate from render state, the exact source-material RGB -> active 256-entry display-palette L1/lowest-index map, and the `RawC3dGpuBackend` trait. The first immutable pipeline profile is deliberately the already-established software-stage opaque COPY path: perspective-correct point/clamp sampling, `D3DCULL_CCW` (clockwise survives), `LessEqual` depth with writes enabled. `cmr-gpu-conformance::PreparedRawStage` walks `StageScene` draw order/active LODs, copies raw entries and compact indices, deduplicates resolved `StageC3dTextures` by ordinal, preserves node WORLD and deferred brackets, and builds VIEW/PROJECTION without CPU transform, clipping, culling, or raster preparation. wgpu and Vulkan SDL_GPU upload those raw streams and perform WORLD/VIEW/PROJECTION, hardware clipping/culling, perspective interpolation, depth, indexed material sampling, exact active-palette resolution, and RGBA8 readback on the GPU. A shared ignored hardware test feeds both implementations the same parsed source-owned C3D+BMP fixture and requires byte-exact readback; it passes on local NVIDIA RTX 5090 and AMD Radeon 610M/RADV selections. SDL-specific culling/depth-order/palette-mutation hardware tests and the wgpu raw-triangle test pass. The contract also defines the persistent prepared-scene boundary: `C3dSceneUpload` locks extent, resource counts, and the active palette as immutable one-time preparation inputs; `C3dPreparedFrame` carries per-frame matrices and validated draw ordinals; and `PersistentRawC3dGpuBackend` separates `prepare_c3d_scene` (all allocation, palette mapping, and upload, fence-completed before return) from `render_prepared_readback` (correctness-only, never ranked) and `render_prepared_timed` (no pixels, no copy/map/readback, fence wait included so submit-to-completion stays comparable). SDL_GPU retains per-mesh vertex/index buffer pairs, per-material source-index and 256-entry L1-map `R8Uint` textures, one shared active-palette `Rgba8Uint` texture, fixed-extent color/depth targets and a padded download buffer, plus a backend-instance token that rejects foreign prepared scenes and poisoning after a failed wait; its hardware test proves identical repeated readbacks, an unchanged resource-operation counter across timed submission, and cross-backend rejection. wgpu retains equivalent buffers/textures plus a strided transform uniform ring consumed through dynamic offsets; its hardware test proves prepared readback equals the synchronous path and that timed frames perform zero resource allocations. | This proves cross-backend correctness for a synthetic raw recovered packet, not a pixel differential against `compose_stage_scene_frame`, final compatibility for every stage material, or performance leadership. Per-corner diffuse/alpha, translucent blending, fog/lighting, truecolor and palette-less engine textures, untextured faces, color-key realization, dynamic LOD, and unresolved effective legacy filtering remain outside the bounded profile. The remaining synchronous `RawC3dGpuBackend` calls still allocate/upload/read back per frame; ranking requires running the new persistent path in a correctness-gated steady-state benchmark over a caller-selected imported stage before I08 can select I09. SDL remains Vulkan/SPIR-V-only and requires external `glslangValidator`; DX12 remains unclaimed. No game payload is included. |
| `impl.gpu-imported-stage-benchmark` | derived | I08's benchmark lane, `cmr-gpu-conformance::stage_benchmark` plus the `cmr-gpu-stage-benchmark` binary. It reads one caller-selected stage's sibling `.c3d`/`.cat` sections in place through an explicit `--source` root (or `CMR_ORIGINAL_DIR`; nothing is copied into the repository), composes the established software stage frame at 640x480 with the application's placeholder grayscale palette and origin camera as the oracle, walks the recovered `StageScene` into `PreparedRawStage`, prepares the persistent GPU scene once per candidate, and ranks only the steady-state `render_prepared_timed` interval (CPU encode + submit, then submit-to-fence completion) after a correctness gate and a caller-set warmup. Correctness is checked before any timing: both candidates must agree byte for byte, and each must match the software oracle within a documented nearest-neighbour **sampling-tie** budget of 0.1% of the frame (307 of 307,200 pixels) - a fixed-point hardware rasterizer cannot reproduce the float `f32`/`f64` software reference exactly where a surface's interpolated UV lands on a texel boundary. The divergence was characterised on the installed media: at every analysed disagreeing pixel the software UV is within 0.11 texel of a boundary and the GPU byte equals the neighbouring texel's active-palette slot (AU1 5 px, NZ2 189 px, SW4 51 px), and the remainder are silhouette-edge coverage flips; no disagreement is a depth-order, culling, palette-mapping, or geometry error (reversing the opaque draw order changes zero oracle pixels, and the candidates are identical to each other on every stage). Corpus result on the installed 60 EU stage scenes: 46 gate on both the NVIDIA RTX 5090 and the AMD 610M/RADV paths (NVIDIA maximum 189 px = 0.062%, RADV maximum 187 px, per-stage candidate disagreements zero, 6 stages exactly equal to the oracle); 12 stages (AU3, CO1, CO6, GR6, IN4, IN5, MC3, SW2, SW3, SW6, UK2, UK4) compose nothing at the placeholder origin camera and are rejected as `empty-scene`, and GR4 still fails texture preparation on the 32x64 padded-layout surface case. The summary is path-free and reports prepared draw/texture counts, per-candidate timing percentiles, oracle mismatch count and maximum channel delta, `cross_backend_exact`, and an explicit `winner=not-selected`. **Captured steady-state timings** (median of three 240-frame runs after 32 warmup frames, microseconds for encode+submit / submit-to-completion): on the NVIDIA RTX 5090, MC5 (1,943 prepared draws) wgpu `1235/98` vs SDL_GPU `3110/91`, NZ2 (1,609) `1296/88` vs `2253/72`, AU1 (1,323) `645/78` vs `1210/59`, IN8 (97) `108/47` vs `66/75`; on the AMD 610M/RADV path, MC5 `1134/283` vs `2360/510`, NZ2 `938/253` vs `1612/445`, AU1 `778/200` vs `1374/165`, IN8 `84/61` vs `43/53`. wgpu therefore leads the draw-heavy recovered stages end to end by roughly 1.7-2.5x (the CPU encode/submit interval dominates, and it scales with prepared draw count), while SDL_GPU leads the 97-draw stage and most completion-only intervals. | This is a derived benchmark/verification lane, not a recovered rendering claim and not a backend selection. The placeholder camera and palette mean the ranked numbers are comparative until the recovered gameplay active palette and a real stage camera are wired; the host also runs unrelated GPU work, so medians of repeated runs were used and the draw-heavy ordering (not the exact values) is the stable result. The 12 camera-invisible stages and the GR4 padded-layout surface bound corpus coverage; no game payload is included. |

| `rea.official-eu.directplay-message-protocol` | observed | Recovery of the game-side message taxonomy carried over the H09 DirectPlay `Send`/`Receive` wrappers (static only). Recovered types and effects: **1** - session/config apply (`FUN_0045ab40`, sets `DAT_00573a28 = 1`, stores the metadata word); **2** - a full race/settings sync copied as `0x168` dwords into the config blob `DAT_00d3ca80` (applies the 3-bit field at `+0x166`, the 4-bit field at `+0x168`, `+0x167`, the `u16` at `+0x6b8`, and the `u16` table lookup at `+0x164`, then calls the race setup routines); **3** and **0x31** - ignored by the non-session handler; **4** - clears `DAT_00573a28`; **5** - player action using `payload[2]` as an id (`FUN_0045baa0`/`FUN_0045b1b0`); **6** - `FUN_004bcfc0(payload + 1, 1)`; **7** - 15 dwords into the current player record (`0x60`-stride table indexed by `FUN_0045b130()`), sets the record ready; **8** - 16 dwords into that record, marks it active and increments its counter; **9** - 17 dwords, stores `DAT_00573a08[i]`/`DAT_00573a34[i]` and marks the record done; **0xa** - stores the metadata word in `DAT_00d3cabc`; **0xb** - sets `DAT_00573a2c`; **0xc** - session-only `FUN_0045a030`/`FUN_0045a090`; **0xd** - clears `DAT_005739b0`; **0xe** - sets `DAT_005739dc`/`DAT_005739f4`; **0x10** - time/position update: `payload[1]` is a player index and `payload[2]` replaces `DAT_005ffa1c[index]` when it is a lower value; **0x11** - 5 dwords, marks the player record and accumulates `payload[1]` into `DAT_00573028`; **0x13** - `FUN_004bbd70`; **0x15** - the 12-byte packet built by `FUN_00431de0` (type + two words) stores `DAT_0054fb80 = payload[2]` and `DAT_004fb98 = payload[1]`; **0x16** - `FUN_0042f590`; **0x101** - start handshake: when not already in a session it initialises the session from the config blob and replies with type **0xa** or **0xb** (16 bytes); any other type falls through to `FUN_004ab7e0`. Send sites build the same shape: `FUN_00431de0` sends type `0x15` with two words (12 bytes), `FUN_004ba900` broadcasts type **1** with `0x27` dwords of the front-end/config blob (160 bytes), and the start handshake replies are 16 bytes. | Static protocol taxonomy, not a complete wire specification: the exact field layouts of the per-player records (types 7/8/9/0x11), the remaining send-side type construction, and the runtime `guidApplication` are still open. No session was created and nothing was executed; the types above are reachable only while a DirectPlay session is open. The types are game constants, not DirectPlay protocol. |
| `impl.tcp-network-transport` | derived | H10's concrete backend: `cmr-core::net_tcp::TcpTransport` implements the recovered `NetworkTransport` contract over `std::net`. The wire format is backend-owned (four-byte magic `CMRN`, version 1, typed frames for hello/descriptor/player/message/bye with a 64 KiB cap) and never leaks into the contract. Player ids are documented backend policy (`HOST_PLAYER` = 2, `CLIENT_PLAYER` = 4) because DirectPlay assigned them dynamically. Three socket tests cover a complete host/join session with two-way message exchange and idempotent teardown, an application-id mismatch, and a descriptor that disagrees with the handshake. | Derived backend evidence, not protocol recovery or multiplayer parity: one host and one peer only, no session discovery, fan-out, security, or lobby; the game-side message payload layouts and synchronization strategy remain unrecovered; and the transport is opt-in (nothing in the playable single-player build opens a socket). Before updating velocity, the routine applies wrapping absolute to cached `+0xec`, Q16-multiplies it by `DAT_004eac74` (literal `0x10000`), scales current velocity by that result, and wrapping-subtracts the vector from accumulated force `+0x51c..+0x524`; inverse-mass scaling by `+0x4c` and wrapping addition of `+0x3cc..+0x3d4` produce acceleration. It then computes `dv=qmul(acceleration,0x3333)`, snapshots old velocity, stores `new_v=old_v+dv`, and later adds `new_v-qmul(dv,0x8000)` to position. The negative-odd half step follows signed Q16 multiplication and arithmetic shift, not truncating integer division. Only after the velocity store is `+0xec` recomputed through the generated legacy square-root path, so damping uses the previous tick's cached speed. The G03-AB rows supersede the earlier open `+0x164/+0x168` limitation and the
reversed store order in the ignored `.work/g03-o-car-schema.md` note. They do
not establish complete-object reconstruction, global writer/consumer coverage,
runtime reachability, or concurrency behavior.

The G03-AA rows supersede G03-W's overbroad “only observed direct store” wording
with “only observed originating constructor assignment,” and supersede later
claims that `FUN_00486090` remained unreconstructed. The caller-owned table and
generic second-caller provenance remain open.

The G03-Z rows supersede only the earlier claim that interval
`0x406324..0x406459` was unreconstructed and the G03-Y represented-stage error
order that lacked the post-helper reciprocal. Complete-object composition and
the immediately preceding `FUN_00486090` effect remain open.

The G03-Y row supersedes the interface descriptions in
`impl.sim-compiled-config-options` and `impl.sim-compiled-stage-effects` that
limited the resolver to three indexed bytes, accepted caller-supplied
post-prefix state `+0x48`, or omitted the prefix from composition. It also
supersedes only the "prefix unreconstructed" limitation in
`rea.official-eu.sim-compiled-stage-dataflow`; the observed static row remains
the authority for instruction behavior and gap boundaries.

The two G03-W config rows above supersede earlier G03-R through G03-V limitation
phrases that called the signed config-index domain wholly unproven. The proven
fact is the `0..=7` domain of every resolved explicit state constructor; the
heterogeneous config object's homogeneous record count remains unproven.

The two G03-X stage rows above supersede earlier milestone limitations that
called composition of every recovered stage absent. G03-Y subsequently adds
the common prefix, and G03-Z adds post-helper interval
`[0x406324,0x40645f)`. G03-AA adds `FUN_00486090` and its caller selection
without embedding the caller-owned table. Complete-object composition is still
unproven.
